View release →

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v19.1.0 Stable2026-11-21T14:32:16Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • Added standby tools support through Agent.setStandbyTools() and AgentContext.standbyTools, allowing tools to remain inactive until a request explicitly enables tool use.

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added provider hooks (setProviderHooks in @oh-my-pi/pi-ai/provider-hooks), which replace a provider's stream adapter or image generation while its models keep their api.
  • Added opt-in prompt perturbation: model-visible prompt prose and tool descriptions use a per-install wording chosen from a committed paraphrase pool covering every static system- and tool-prompt line. Models enable it with the prompt-perturbation KDL axis; OMP_PROMPT_PERTURBATION=1/0 forces it on or off.

Changed

  • The Cursor provider forwards bash instead of write so staged previews can be finalized
  • Increased provider stream watchdog defaults from 5 to 15 minutes, preventing slow one-shot completions from being interrupted prematurely.
  • Extended Gemini reasoning-stream protection to allow longer planning sequences before interruption.

Fixed

  • Broker-backed credential stores now detect a login the broker stores but does not serve and keep it in the local agent.db (HybridAuthCredentialStore) instead of losing it on the next snapshot; a broker-only RemoteAuthCredentialStore fails the upload with AuthBrokerUnservedProviderError.
  • OpenAI Codex requests no longer send the session_id and conversation_id headers; the session goes out only as session-id, like the Codex CLI, so proxies that reject underscore header names (Envoy, nginx defaults) pass them (#20018)
  • Preserved the intended conversation structure when a user message follows a tool result for models that fold user messages after tool results.
  • LiteLLM requests now rotate to another configured API key when a 422 budget-exceeded response disables the current key. (#19860)
  • GPT-6.2 system prompts are now sent as leading developer messages through third-party Responses proxies, enabling prompt caching and avoiding unnecessary full-rate input charges on subsequent turns. (openai/codex#62312)
  • Improved thinking-mode detection across request formats, avoiding unnecessary retries and correctly remembering formats accepted or rejected by the server. (#19758 by @eggpeat)
  • Auth refresh failures that could cause an access token to expire before the next retry are now surfaced with a warning instead of remaining visible only in debug logs. (#19590 by @will-bogusz)
  • Chat Completions now preserve reasoning returned only in reasoning_details, including reasoning from Bifrost Gemini. (#19595 by @Xytronix)
  • Antigravity quota exhaustion is now reported as a 429 rate-limit error, allowing configured retries, credential rotation, and model fallback behavior to apply correctly instead of treating the response as assistant text. (#19382 by @gimso2x)
  • Anthropic requests now preserve prompt caching when tools are activated later in a conversation, avoiding unnecessary system-prompt and transcript cache rewrites. (#19826)

@oh-my-pi/browser-relay

Breaking Changes

  • The omp extension now uses a pinned Chrome extension ID and connects to the browser relay through its native host. Reinstall the extension using Chrome’s “Load unpacked” flow; the relay port and token options have been removed.

Added

  • Added an omp Chrome side panel with session model selection, role shortcuts, thinking controls, input shortcuts, message queuing, and run interruption controls.
  • Added browser interaction features including page overlays, image and element picking, Teach, scheduled shortcuts, and GIF export.

Changed

  • The Chrome side panel shows brief labels for tool steps with structured intents.
  • The extension now keeps registered installations up to date across omp upgrades and reloads when idle.
  • Browser relay ports are assigned automatically and discovered locally by terminal clients, while sidebar chats continue using their existing connection.
  • Side panel sessions now control the connected Chrome browser directly instead of opening a separate headless browser.

Fixed

  • Improved relay recovery so Chrome reconnects automatically when the native host is unavailable or a stale connection drops.

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Breaking Changes

  • Removed the built-in Gemini CLI OAuth provider (google-gemini-cli). Google Antigravity and Antigravity CLI remain available.

Added

  • Added runtime provider rules (setRuntimeProviderRule in compat/cascade). They apply KDL directives to one provider's models, win ties with bundled rules, and clear rule-derived caches when they change.
  • Added the prompt-perturbation catalog axis, which opts a provider or model into per-install prompt paraphrasing.
  • Added the uncached-instructions compatibility flag for GPT-6.2 models across all hosts, moving system prompts out of the uncached Responses instructions field.

Fixed

  • Fixed Vercel AI Gateway model refreshes so public models remain discoverable when a stored API key has expired.
  • Fixed OpenRouter models that require reasoning but do not advertise effort levels; requests that disable thinking now use the lowest supported reasoning effort instead of failing.
  • Fixed Gemini 4 models (Argon, Carbon, Hydrogen, and Neon) failing with empty malformed responses when messages arrived immediately after tool results.
  • Updated pricing for newly served Cursor models to use their documented per-token rates instead of being reported as free.
  • Fixed Codex client version caching so its cache file is stored under the configured $XDG_CACHE_HOME/omp directory.

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • Replaced xd:// tool devices with tool commands: rarely-used tools now run as bash commands (<tool> --help, <tool> <args>) while bash is active and are ordinary tools otherwise
  • Renamed the tools.xdev, tools.xdevDocs, tools.xdevInlineDevices and tools.xdevNotices settings to tools.commands, tools.commandDocs, tools.commandInline and tools.commandNotices
  • Staged previews, plan submission and Auto-QA reports now use the resolve, reject, propose and report_issue commands, and tool doc topics moved from xd://<tool>/<topic> to help://<tool>/<topic>
  • Removed the tui.completeScrollback setting; transcript rows covered by the HUD, dialogs, or draft now always move to scrollback, and the input returns to the bottom row when overlays close.
  • Replaced the standalone MCP transport and tool-cache SDK exports and removed DeferredMCPTool plus MCP cache and reconnect-policy manager options. MCP servers are now accessed through the MCP host APIs.
  • Browser relay hosting now runs through the omp extension's native host while Chrome is open. Removed the on-demand omp browser-relay server command and its --port, --token, and --no-group options; browser.relayUrl remains available for relays forwarded from another machine. Reload the omp extension once after upgrading, or restart Chrome.
  • Changed archive.search(query) to search session titles, idle recaps, and user/assistant content across all history and return matching session records; use archive.prompts({ query }) for prompt-only searches. Searches match query terms at the beginning of words.
  • RPC hosts must handle the ask tool as one complete HTML-form form request and respond with submitted data or cancellation; field-by-field prompts are no longer emitted.

Added

  • Extensions can take over a built-in provider through registerProvider: stream replaces its request adapter, generateImage its image generation, and webSearch its grounded search request, and discoverModels its model discovery request. rules sets KDL rule directives (such as prompt-perturbation) for its models.
  • Added the magic edit mode, which accepts Markdown bulleted change descriptions grouped under # path headings and uses a configurable fast model or role to generate and apply patches. It supports concise block replacement, deletion, movement, and identifier-renaming descriptions, and presents the resulting diffs.
  • Added remote session model controls through RPC, including the model picker, thinking-level and role selection, auto thinking, and commands to resume, abort while delivering queued messages, or continue the host agent. Collab guests can now continue, interrupt, and queue follow-up prompts for the host.
  • Added support for Unicode set mode (v) in slash-delimited secrets.yml regular expressions.
  • Moved MCP servers into the omp daemon, with optional process sharing across sessions, lazy startup, idle shutdown, shared discovery caching, and /mcp list status including process and session information. Configure sharing with mcp.shared and idle shutdown with mcp.idleStopMinutes.
  • Added MCP protocol revision 2026-07-28 support, including server discovery, per-request metadata, root input rounds, subscriptions, and cache controls, while retaining compatibility with servers that require the initialize handshake.
  • Added native browser-relay integration for the Chrome extension, including side-panel sessions, extension installation and synchronization, automatic profile registration, and in-tab action overlays. Side-panel sessions remain separate from terminal resume, recent-session, --continue, and ACP session lists unless explicitly resumed.
  • Added a coloured extension-status variant to omp gallery --segment status.
  • Added notifications and waiting status when a program running in a bash pty is waiting for keyboard input, such as a password or confirmation prompt; disable notifications with pty.notify.
  • Eval completion() now records raw provider frames for /debug inspection and saves frames from failed calls as log files in the session artifacts directory.
  • Added parentToolCallId to extension tool_call and tool_result events so nested tool calls can be associated with the eval cell or kernel-defined tool that dispatched them.
  • Added full-text transcript search indexing for archive.search, covering user and assistant messages across session history.
  • Session files now record approval requests and their outcomes, allowing dashboards and sidebars to show when a session is waiting for tool approval.
  • Added live visibility into output redirected to files by background bash jobs, including job streams, the jobs sheet, job widgets, and command cards; selecting a command shows the output for its relevant pipeline or redirected stream.
  • Added Binary Ninja as a disassembler backend for read analysis views and the dsm namespace. Select it with disassembler.backend = binja and configure its installation with binja.installDir or $BN_INSTALL_DIR.
  • Clicking the usage-limit segment in native terminal hosts such as Tern now opens /usage. Changing Tern's Agents tray setting immediately switches between tray cards and the running-agent and job counters without restarting omp.

Changed

  • Images a command prints as kitty or sixel graphics are saved to a temp file and linked in the bash output, in addition to being attached
  • generate_image returns its images as attachments
  • Spotlight tool calls use Label: detail; activity shows only the label, while output ranking uses the full intent.
  • Improved regex performance for TTSR and secret-redaction scans, including SIMD acceleration and faster candidate matching, without changing matching semantics.
  • Improved performance when listing sessions across all projects, especially with thousands of project directories.
  • Interactive wait now waits up to four minutes on its first call for background jobs instead of returning after five seconds.
  • Updated the MCP OAuth client name to Codex.
  • Made automatic retries less disruptive: failed attempts stay out of the transcript, retry status explains the reason, and successful recovery leaves only a brief indication.
  • Selective commits now attribute edit-tool and shell-built-in changes more accurately, distinguish external program changes for review, and use a substantially smaller change journal. Long-line commit previews now highlight only the changed words while preserving the original patch.
  • TTSR rules that cannot interrupt streaming are evaluated at the end of the relevant response, reasoning, or tool call, reducing unnecessary work while preserving matches and reminders.
  • Eval completion() now accepts user-tagged model agents, such as models pinned with ^model, and uses their tagged effort level.
  • read recognizes SQLite-backed .i64 and .idb files as IDA databases and reports disassembler-backend mismatches clearly.

Fixed

  • A tool card left unfinished by a stream that never completed no longer keeps the rest of the transcript out of scrollback and squeezed to one-line rows; it is closed when the next reply starts
  • Spotlight keeps short relevant passages from multiple large function bodies together with their signatures, even when a code outline is cut again.
  • A usage-limit reset inside retry.maxDelayMs is now waited out even without a Retry-After header.
  • Logging in while an auth broker is configured no longer reports success for a provider the broker stores but does not serve (such as one an older broker pauses); the login is kept in this machine's agent.db instead, survives restarts, and login/logout now name where credentials were saved.
  • Spotlight keeps more high-relevance lines with their neighbours in long results and large code outlines, while preserving visible intent matches within the window budget.
  • Fixed Tern tray cards and subagent live previews showing stale calls, models, or thinking states while an agent was running or after a model switch; completed cards now retain the model and thinking level used.
  • Fixed extension side-panel sessions launching omp's own browser instead of driving only the user's Chrome window.
  • Preserved answer footers and Copy controls in Tern after resuming, compacting, or rebuilding a transcript.
  • Prevented --continue and autoResume from attaching to a session currently being written by another omp process.
  • Added visible progress reporting while /handoff writes its summary.
  • Fixed access to cloud-only files on macOS iCloud Drive, Desktop & Documents, Google Drive, and Dropbox locations.
  • Prevented pathological TTSR condition and secret-redaction regular expressions from freezing sessions or requests.
  • Fixed prompt-cache handling for IRC-woken subagents and sessions whose context hooks provide an initial message.
  • Improved shell rg and fd path output and option handling to match their native tools, including paths beginning with -, null-separated output, and explicit current-directory prefixes.
  • Fixed sloppy-edit validation and block matching for incomplete actions, numeric || expressions, nested or partial block endings, JSDoc comments, and hashline headers with trailing whitespace.
  • Fixed OpenCode v2 MCP configuration discovery from mcp.servers while preserving support for legacy flat mcp entries.
  • Fixed ovnfs commands when ovnfs.path contains Windows backslashes, spaces, or $ characters.
  • Stopped failed browser launches from logging URL credentials, query strings, OAuth device codes, or fragments.
  • Fixed web_search to honor the search order and exclusions configured for the current session.
  • Fixed MCP OAuth refresh for Microsoft Entra servers where the client is also the resource; previously saved credentials may require one /mcp reauth.
  • Fixed model resolution for OpenRouter route variants used by per-call subagents and roles.
  • Fixed secret redaction in shared sessions so provider-encrypted reasoning is not retained unexpectedly.
  • Fixed bash approval handling for compound commands with environment assignments and flags, including wildcard allow rules.
  • Fixed selective commits when HEAD changes during a commit hook and when edits from multiple writers are reordered or overlap; ambiguous changes are now sent to review instead of being duplicated or silently misapplied.
  • Fixed archive records to include all documented keys with null for missing values.
  • Fixed unnecessary empty session log files for inline bash/eval output and request-timeout recovery.
  • Improved read suggestions for line, range, and column selectors and preserved source line metadata for in-memory and converted text reads.
  • Fixed agent lifecycle races involving duplicate IDs, pending messages, shutdown, and revival.
  • Fixed browser tab handling on CDP engines that use separate connections per page, including recovery after timeouts or worker exits.
  • Fixed browser interaction reporting for overlay-only clicks, hidden frames containing inline SVG or MathML, and search fields that update on key events.
  • Fixed browser computer-use inspection of minimized macOS windows and fully covered Windows Chrome, Edge, and Electron windows.
  • Fixed /goal on Anthropic from unnecessarily rewriting the cached system prompt and transcript.
  • Fixed isolated task subagents in bare Git repositories by refusing early and identifying available work trees instead of starting without a usable work tree.
  • Fixed Nix browser-relay builds to fetch all dependencies without falling back to the npm registry.
  • Fixed extension, plugin, custom-tool, hook, command, and share-script loading for paths containing ?, with a useful error identifying the path and remedy.

@oh-my-pi/collab-web

Added

  • Enhanced guest collaboration APIs: GuestClient.sendPrompt now supports sending images, while sendResume, sendDeliverQueue, and the followUp option enable continuing the host’s agent, interrupting it to deliver queued messages, or queueing a follow-up prompt.

Changed

  • Tool commands run inside a bash call render as nested cards under the bash card
  • Tool cards in shared sessions and HTML exports show brief intent labels instead of detailed ranking instructions.

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Improved diagnostics when a configured embedding provider fails, making provider errors visible in logs while preserving the existing fallback behavior.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • Added host-provided session commands to Shell.run (commands) and shellReservedCommandNames()

Fixed

  • Fixed grouped sed scripts such as 1{h;d};2{G} being rejected when no semicolon precedes a closing brace.
  • Fixed the native addon failing to load when HOME, PI_NATIVES_DIR, or the installation path contains ?.
  • commitCreate() now reports a HeadMoved error when HEAD changes after the expected-HEAD check, including changes made by a commit-msg hook.
  • Fixed macOS window reads for minimized windows so the requested window remains inspectable without being restored or replaced by a sibling window; unavailable menus and commands now report WindowUnavailable.
  • Fixed Windows captures and accessibility trees incorrectly reporting fully covered Chromium and Electron windows as current; they now report covered when those applications stop drawing while covered.
  • Fixed Windows computer setValue bringing classic Win32 and WinForms text-box windows to the foreground and directing subsequent keyboard input into them.

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Breaking Changes

  • Retired the ComposerPreferences.completeScrollback, TranscriptContainer.completeScrollback, and Composer.setRunActive APIs, along with the transientChrome, selfClearingChrome, and billedChrome options of Composer.setRuntimeChildren. Retirement now consistently uses the height left by on-screen chrome.
  • Moved convertImageToPng, convertImageToPngShared, cachedPngConversion, and imagePayloadKey from @oh-my-pi/pi-tui/chat/image-loading to @oh-my-pi/pi-tui/png-conversion.
  • Replaced OutputSinkOptions.artifactPath and artifactId with the allocateArtifact (ArtifactAllocator) callback, which allocates an artifact only when output first exceeds its inline view.
  • Removed the windows-altgr module; Windows AltGr recovery is now provided by translateWindowsAltGrSequence in @oh-my-pi/pi-natives.

Added

  • The editor now displays described changes for magic mode as Markdown in the transcript while it waits for the apply model to return final diffs.
  • The UI now responds to terminal features events without waiting for a new hello message.

Changed

  • Tool commands run inside a bash call render as nested tool cards in the bash card
  • Tool activity and job titles show the brief label of a structured intent; the Spotlight inspector still exposes the full request.
  • Improved transcript layout around docked reports, waiting asks, completion lists, dialogs, todo lists, subagent panels, queued messages, and other lower-screen chrome so visible content and reclaimed rows behave more predictably.
  • Native chat and web search cards now present tool results and sources more clearly, including expandable page excerpts, multi-line titles, and a unified sources grid.
  • Windows console VT input and AltGr handling now use the native terminal integration.
  • Background jobs and running Bash commands now provide live, step-specific output for pipelines, redirects, and command stages, with controls to switch streams or follow the live output. Job cards also show the active command step and more compact metadata.
  • Bash command footers now use a compact, icon-based layout for timing, exit status, job and process details, artifacts, and minimizers.

Fixed

  • Fixed the TUI freezing at 100% CPU while streaming text that begins with a blank line followed by a line containing |.
  • Fixed the TUI freezing when charting a table whose numbers overflow (1e400, a p999… header with hundreds of digits, a 1e-300 → 1e300 change) or sit above 2^53 and differ only slightly.
  • Fixed the editor freezing when a vim count is typed with hundreds of digits; counts now cap at 99,999.
  • Fixed Windows input mode freezing on a key record with an impossibly large repeat count.
  • Fixed backward character jump (Ctrl+Alt+]) at the start of a line finding the cursor's own character instead of searching earlier lines.
  • Fixed light-theme detection inside Zellij when the terminal reports a light background.
  • Preserved custom foreground colors and text styles in status-line segments while continuing to apply the accent color to unstyled statuses.
  • Fixed transcript rows and tool cards disappearing or appearing truncated when overlays and panels were open.
  • Fixed cursor flicker in kitty terminals during spinner updates.
  • Preserved background colors on truncated rows while scrolling.
  • Fixed WebP and JPEG images rendering as blank gaps on Kitty graphics-compatible terminals when displayed through a bare Image.
  • Improved terminal output handling so large repaints remain row-safe and slow or unavailable terminal writers no longer freeze input.
  • Preserved prefilled or hidden __proto__ controls when ask HTML forms time out.
  • A single-question ask form now submits on the first Enter press.
  • Improved native-terminal ask forms so answering long questions no longer causes the entire card to resize and stall on every movement.

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added LinearRegExp (@oh-my-pi/pi-utils/regex), a JavaScript-compatible regular expression engine that avoids catastrophic backtracking, with support for scoped modifiers, Unicode modes and properties, Unicode set operations, large repetitions and literal dictionaries, fixed-width block counts, and additional character classes.
  • Added RegexStream for detecting regular-expression matches in text received incrementally in chunks.
  • Added getCodexClientVersionPath() to locate the Codex client version cache record under the initialized $XDG_CACHE_HOME/omp directory.

Changed

  • Improved LinearRegExp performance for scans and searches involving delimiters, word backreferences, required literals or suffixes, lookbehinds, and large character alphabets.

Fixed

  • Fixed RAR4 extraction stalling for seconds per filter on archives that declare an out-of-range delta filter channel count; such archives are now rejected as corrupt.
  • Fixed formatAge to display durations of one year or more in years, such as 23y ago, instead of months.

@oh-my-pi/pi-wire

Added

  • Added toolIntentLabel for displaying the brief prefix of a structured tool-call intent.
  • Added clickable shell-command steps with output selection and visual highlighting of the currently displayed step.
  • Added a terminal features event for notifying clients when terminal settings change.
  • Added collaboration guest support for resuming sessions and delivering queued messages, plus followUp prompts.

kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill ↗ 13 releases · 13 stable
v4.4.1 Stable2026-11-21T13:52:30Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.4.0.

Fixed

  • A position given in milliseconds is its frame in freeze.py, broll.py and speedramp.py too (#653). At 30000/1001, freeze.py --at 1.902 held the frame after the one at 1.9019 s, broll.py --at 1.902 began the cutaway a frame late, and a speedramp.py segment ending at 1.902 changed speed a frame late. The three now read the time as cut.py does since 4.3.6: the nearest frame when it is within half a millisecond.

Changed

  • SKILL.md: one ordinary edit is one call. The workflow's eight steps (probe, dry-run, run, check, verify, look) read as the path for every request, and a model took a plain cut or resize through all of them. A paragraph now says which requests need one call with --json and which need the steps (a delivery, a long or overwriting job, an edit that changes what the picture shows). Nine routing rows are merged into their neighbours to stay inside the 220-line budget; no request loses its row.
  • fix: a position given in milliseconds is its frame in freeze, broll and speedramp too (#672)
  • docs(skill): one ordinary edit is one call (#670)

What's Changed

  • docs(skill): one ordinary edit is one call by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/670
  • fix: a position given in milliseconds is its frame in freeze, broll and speedramp too by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/672

v4.4.0 Stable2026-11-21T11:25:38Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.3.6.

Added

  • audio.py --mute: the sound is removed in one call. The picture and subtitles are copied; nothing is re-encoded.
  • fit.py --fade-in S --fade-out S: a fade from and to black, picture and sound together. The fade-out ends at the output's length, with --duration too. audio.py --fade-in/--fade-out stays the sound-only fade.
  • export.py --duration S: a delivery of the first S seconds, for "a Reel of the first 15 seconds" (--preset reels --fit crop --duration 15). --max-mb sizes for the shorter length.

Changed

  • A 9:16 frame smaller than a delivery frame is said. fit.py --aspect 9:16 on a 1280x720 clip keeps the source's size and writes 406x720, as before; the result now says so and names --width 1080 for the usual 1080x1920. SKILL.md's "make it vertical" row asks for it.
  • An enlargement is Lanczos. When fit.py or an export.py preset writes a frame larger than the picture, the scale uses -sws_flags lanczos; a frame of the source's size or smaller is the command it was.
  • feat: a small 9:16 frame is said, an enlargement is Lanczos, and mute, fade and a delivery length are one call (#669)

What's Changed

  • feat: a small 9:16 frame is said, an enlargement is Lanczos, and mute, fade and a delivery length are one call by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/669

v4.3.6 Stable2026-11-21T10:16:28Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.3.5.

Fixed

  • MCP on Windows: a tools/call is answered while the client keeps its pipe open (#664). From 3.10.0 the response was written only when the client closed stdin: the tool ran and wrote its file, but its process had inherited the server's stdin (the client's JSON-RPC pipe) and did not finish exiting while that stayed open. A client that waits for the answer waited for ever. Every child this skill starts now gets its own stdin. Reported, traced and fixed in one word by @haikal2715.
  • A time given in milliseconds is cut on its frame (#653). At 30000/1001, cut.py --start 1.902 kept one frame fewer than --start 1.9019 and --end 1.902 one more: a boundary rounded to the millisecond fell just after its frame. cut.py now cuts a start or end on the nearest frame when that frame is within half a millisecond; requested_start and requested_end stay what was asked. By @zfareed.
  • Twenty options have their help text (#558). --help showed twenty options of graphics.py, join.py, look.py, multicam.py, overlay.py, silence.py and others with nothing after the name; each now says what it takes, and a test refuses an option without help. By @zfareed.
  • fix: MCP on Windows answers a tool call, a millisecond time is cut on its frame, twenty options have help (#668)

What's Changed

  • fix: MCP on Windows answers a tool call, a millisecond time is cut on its frame, twenty options have help by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/668

v4.3.1 Stable2026-11-21T04:29:19Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.3.0.

Fixed

  • FFmpeg 9.1: a tool that writes AAC no longer runs until its time limit (#659). 9.1 made a new coder the AAC encoder's default, and on some sound it never returns: sync.py --replace-audio with a second file starting 0.1 s or 2.5 s late (mono, 192k) left a 48-byte file on Windows with FFmpeg 9.1.0. From 9.1 on, every AAC track is written with the coder that was the default until then (-aac_coder twoloop, visible in the plan); on older builds the commands are unchanged. A command that names a coder keeps it.
  • fix: from FFmpeg 9.1 on, AAC is written with the coder that was the default until then (#660)

What's Changed

  • fix: from FFmpeg 9.1 on, AAC is written with the coder that was the default until then by @kajisho5 in https://github.com/kajisho5/ffmpeg-skill/pull/660

v4.3.0 Stable2026-11-21T01:05:44Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.2.2.

  • A wrong call is answered with the corrected command. Where every mistake on the line has one answer, the usage error ends with try: and the whole command, ready to copy: denoise.py --strength strong gets --strength high, silence.py --threshold -30dB --min-silence 0.8s gets --threshold=-30 --min-silence 0.8, cut.py --strat 1 gets --start 1. Nothing is suggested when a mistake has no single answer.
  • Three things most models asked for. proxy.py --height 360 (the width follows), redact.py --rect 100:40:180:60 (the rectangle in one value) and metadata.py --chapter 0:00=Intro --chapter 1:30=Main (chapters without a file). Each came from most of nine models given the same tasks blind.
  • tests/first_try.py gives a blind call a two-minute clip and real files for the names it invents (b.mp4, music.mp3, a.srt, logo.png), so a refusal is about the call. The strongest model's calls that run on the first try read 48 of 126 with it (39 before).
  • feat: the corrected command after a wrong call, and three options most models asked for (#656)

v4.2.2 Stable2026-11-20T21:39:37Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.2.1.

  • An output that ffmpeg would split into files of its own naming is refused. -o out.m3u8 wrote out0.ts, -o out.mpd wrote init-stream0.m4s and -o frame%03d.png wrote frame001.png onward; files of those names that already existed were replaced without --overwrite, and the tool failed only afterwards (#562). Every tool now refuses such a name before anything runs, dry runs included, as output_extension_unsupported. A literal percent in a name (100%.mp4) and look.py's single image are written as before. Thanks to @zfareed, whose #568 worked out which names ffmpeg expands.

v4.1.3 Stable2026-11-20T15:22:50Z

Automated release: version and notes generated from pull requests merged since 4.1.2.

  • Edge values are probed, and the probe is a skill. tests/edge_values.py gives every numeric option of the single-input tools a zero, a negative and a huge value in a dry run and lists the calls that ran or hung; .claude/skills/edge-probe/SKILL.md has the loop, with the few properties a tool should keep. The first round is #645: 558 calls, 344 refused, two options that hang on a huge value. Nothing in the tools changes here.
  • A number outside what an option takes is refused, on every tool. cropdetect.py --samples 1000000000 and graphics.py --template countdown --from 1000000000 gave no answer; caption.py --size -1 and overlay.py --font-size 1000000000 wrote a file; fit.py --width 1000000000 failed inside ffmpeg with a message that named no option (#645). One shared step now reads every numeric option: a range the help states as a..b is the range taken, a negative is refused unless the help names one (a level in dB, an angle, -1 = never), and no value is past a million (a sample count aside). The answer names the option and prints its help. Ranges that were missing are in the help: cropdetect --samples 1..100, graphics --from 0..600, overlay --font-size 1..2000, audio --gain -96..96, the widths of fit, look, proxy and waveform up to 16384; scenes --threshold is 0..100 as its help said. The same probe that found 213 accepted edge values now finds 104, each one a value its help describes.
  • fix: a number outside what its option takes is refused on every tool (#648)
  • docs: edge values are probed by tests/edge_values.py, and the probe is a skill (#646)

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v4.0.38CC Switch v4.0.38 Stable2026-11-21T13:35:14Z

CC Switch v4.0.38

本版本以修复为主。一键导入链接现在可以导入 WorkBuddy 供应商；Linux 上也能在授权中心切换 Antigravity 账号；Antigravity 的会话列表不再混进空会话和内部子会话，会话阅读器能看到思考、工具调用和结果。

另外修复了 DeepSeek Harness 的配置文件经过某些 YAML 工具后，所有第三方供应商都消失；从访达打开 CC Switch 时，「应用」页把能正常用的 npm 安装标成「无法运行」；技能仓库不是 GitHub / GitLab / Gitea 时只提示「0 个技能」而不报错；Codex 给模型选的思考等级有时没生效；Codex 经路由的几处格式转换错误；用量重复计算；会话正文搜索失败时显示「没有会话包含这个词」；主界面变成一条白条或被 macOS 菜单栏遮住；Linux AppImage 里点链接没反应等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

WorkBuddy 支持一键导入链接

ccswitch:// 供应商链接现在可以导入 WorkBuddy 供应商（#9768），生成的卡片和在 WorkBuddy 页手动添加的一样。

  • 链接必须带模型；apiFormat 只支持 openai_chat。
  • 模型 ID 已被别的卡片占用时，导入会被拒绝，不会留下半截卡片。

Linux 上切换 Antigravity 账号

Linux 上也能在授权中心管理 agy 的登录、切换账号，官方卡片上显示所绑定账号的额度（#9774，相关 #9245）。

  • 登录凭据存在系统的钥匙串（Secret Service）里。钥匙串锁着时，后台查状态和额度会跳过，主动切号时会请求解锁。
  • Linux 上绑了账号的卡片，「打开终端」对话框里多了「本次使用 agy 当前登录」：本机没有绑定的账号或钥匙串不可用时，可以先用当前登录打开终端，卡片的绑定保留不变。

变更

  • Antigravity 会话列表和会话阅读器（#9266，见 #9265）：在终端里只查一下 /usage 就退出留下的空会话（显示为 Jan 1）、已删除或从别的设备同步来的残留记录、/boost 子代理和 /btw 旁路提问不再出现在列表里，它们的用量照常统计。没有标题的会话用预览或第一句提问当标题。会话阅读器能看到思考、正文、工具调用（含 MCP、浏览器等）和工具结果（含命令退出码、中断状态），文件改动显示增删行数。升级后会话正文索引会自动重建一次。
  • 会话搜索和用量统计更快（#9823、#9825）：会话页切换应用时不再为每个会话 ID 和路径重建搜索索引，内存占用也小得多；用量统计的查询能用上数据库索引，首字时间只读一遍，价格每次查询只加载一次。
  • 授权中心里 Google 账号的套餐名去掉多余的「Google」（#9834）：Antigravity 和 Gemini CLI 账号的「Google AI Pro」现在显示为「AI Pro」。

修复

  • DeepSeek Harness 里所有第三方供应商突然消失（见 #9785）：cordis.patch.yml 经过某些 YAML 工具保存后，思考档位里的 off 会变成 false。CC Switch 以前会原样存下来、每次同步都写回去，DeepSeek Harness 因为这一个非法键把整段供应商配置都判为无效。现在读写时都会改正（false 改回 off，不认识的档位去掉）。
  • 从访达打开 CC Switch 时，「应用」页把能正常用的 npm 安装标成「无法运行」（见 #9810）：检测安装时用的 PATH 里找不到 node，用 npm --prefix、volta、pnpm、bun 等方式装的命令行工具就会被判成无法运行，界面还会建议重装或卸载。现在检测时带上登录 shell 的 PATH，和 CC Switch 运行其他命令行工具时一样。Windows 不受影响。
  • 添加不支持的站点（如 cnb.cool）上的技能仓库时，只提示「0 个技能」而不报错（见 #9812）：这类站点对任何地址都返回一个网页，以前下载到的网页被当成压缩包，失败了也没有提示。现在会提示「下载到的不是压缩包：这个站点可能不是 GitHub / GitLab / Gitea 仓库，暂不支持」。
  • Codex 给模型手动选的思考等级（如 max、ultra）有时没生效（#9017，见 #8952）：模型名和 Codex 官方模型对得上时，生成的模型目录用的是官方的思考等级。现在按卡片里选的写入。
  • Codex 经路由转成 Chat 格式时，名字相近的工具会撞名（#9554，见 #9551）：工具名或命名空间里自带 __ 时，以前可能丢掉工具，或者把调用发给错的工具。现在每个工具都有唯一的名字，调用能准确对回去。
  • Codex 经路由用过第三方模型的对话，切到官方模型后无法继续（#9756，见 #9054）：转换出来的消息 ID 以 resp_ 开头，官方接口要求以 msg_ 开头，直连模式下会报 invalid_id_prefix。新生成的 ID 改为 msg_ 开头；已经存下的旧对话不变。
  • Codex 经路由连 AWS Bedrock 上的 Claude 时，每个请求都报 400（#8659，见 #6118）：工具定义里多带了 OpenAI 专用的 strict 字段，Bedrock 不接受。现在不再转发这个字段。
  • 用量重复计算：
    • 模型名带网关前缀（如 antigravity/gemini-3.8-flash-high）时，同一个请求在路由记录和 Codex 会话日志里各算一次（#9249）。现在去掉前缀再比对。
    • Gemini 旧会话文件更新后，已经归档的用量又被导入一次；会话里的 token 数变了却没有更新（#9527，见 #9523）。
  • 用量统计里 Codex 的缓存写入显示 N/A（#8191）：现在显示实际数值，只有 Gemini 和 Grok Build 仍显示 N/A（它们不上报缓存写入）。请求日志也不再把 Codex 的缓存写入算进新输入。
  • Antigravity 的输出速度显示为估算值，偏低（#9788，见 #9787）：Antigravity 自己记录了首字时间和流式输出时长，以前没用上，速度按总耗时（含首字前的等待）估算。现在和经路由的请求一样按实际输出时长计算。
  • 会话正文搜索出错时，显示「没有会话包含这个词」（#9512，见 #9511）：现在会提示「正文搜索失败，暂时只能按标题、目录和首末消息查找」，可以点「重试」，重试时会把会话重新收录进索引。
  • OpenCode 会话的两个边界问题（#7982）：迁移到新格式后又在 OpenCode 1.x 里继续的会话，以前显示的是旧内容；新格式迁移中途中断时，没迁完的会话不再被隐藏。
  • Oh My Pi 的恢复会话命令在 Windows 上粘贴到 PowerShell 会报错（#9153，见 #9152）：Windows 上现在生成 PowerShell 写法，macOS 和 Linux 不变。
  • Linux AppImage 里点链接在浏览器中打开、或打开文件夹，没有任何反应（#9113，见 #9112）：AppImage 自带的旧版 xdg-open 在 KDE Plasma 6 下什么都不做。现在改用系统自己的 xdg-open。deb / rpm、macOS 和 Windows 不受影响。
  • macOS 上把全屏的主界面关到托盘，再打开时被菜单栏和 Dock 遮住（#9770，见 #9769）：现在关到托盘前先退出全屏，从托盘、Dock 等再打开时恢复全屏。
  • 主界面打开后只是一条白条（#9504，见 #9496）：记住的窗口尺寸异常小时，打开时恢复成默认大小。
  • 编辑供应商时，关掉还在保存的编辑器再打开，「保存」按钮一直转圈（#9593，见 #9592）。
  • Copilot 旧版登录迁移遇到网络超时就放弃，要重启才会再试（#9549，见 #9544）：现在网络恢复后，下次用到 Copilot 时会继续迁移，不用重启；只有令牌失效（401 / 403）或退出登录才放弃。
  • 中转站地址被误认成官方厂商（#9648）：地址的路径或参数里出现官方域名，或者域名长得像（如 api.x.ai.example.com）时，以前会被当成那家厂商：xAI 走专门的处理，余额和套餐额度也按那家的官方模板查。现在只看真正的域名。
  • 导入配置、云同步下载等操作中，累加式应用有供应商没写进配置文件，却提示成功（#9747）：现在会列出写入失败的供应商。
  • 移动技能存储位置后，有应用没能重新链接，却提示成功（#9748）：现在会在提示里列出来。

致谢

感谢 @thisTom（WorkBuddy 一键导入链接）、@Railgun-wiki（Linux 上切换 Antigravity 账号、Antigravity 会话）、@jimpablo（Antigravity 会话）、@YUZHEthefool（会话搜索和用量统计提速）、@StrengW（Codex 思考等级）、@HaiYangBG1（Codex 工具撞名、Copilot 登录迁移、Gemini 用量重复计算）、@Logic-Bin（Codex 消息 ID）、@zihenghe04（Bedrock 的 strict 字段）、@AkimioJR（网关前缀用量去重）、@Chaoqi31（Codex 缓存写入）、@lyfuci（Antigravity 输出速度）、@jzcangshu（会话正文搜索失败提示）、@ISuuuu（OpenCode 会话）、@wlndysky（Oh My Pi 恢复命令）、@NeoChen1024（AppImage 打开链接）、@fuyundev（macOS 全屏窗口）、@1clipse（主界面白条）、@MonsterXia（编辑器保存转圈）、@SaladDay（中转地址误认、失败提示、代码整理）、@funkpopo、@allenxu09（评审修改）的贡献。

特别感谢 @kense-lab：你的 PR 发现并修复了 Codex 经路由连 Bedrock 时 strict 字段被拒的问题，只是同样的问题后来用另一种改法修好，PR 因此关闭。

感谢 @zhmmartin、@308521201-stack（同时给出了修法）、@Gitzzr、@Duosl、@LyciumRuthenicum、@AlanQi1 报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

v4.0.37CC Switch v4.0.37 Stable2026-11-20T15:10:02Z

CC Switch v4.0.37

本版本以修复为主。读不出来的供应商和 MCP 条目（比如由更新版本写入、经 WebDAV / S3 同步过来的）不再被当成空配置、下次保存时被默认值覆盖，而是单独列出来，可以删除；Pi 新增 Mistral AI 预设；韩语界面翻译整体改进。

另外修复了本地路由监听地址填 IPv6 时启动不了；「应用到 Claude Code 插件」会把损坏的 ~/.claude/config.json 整个清空；网速慢时大的技能仓库下载到一半被中断；从访达打开 CC Switch 时 Codex 模型目录探测找不到 Node；工具调用参数里 _ 开头的字段被删掉后才发给上游；移出又加回故障转移队列的供应商仍被跳过；插件页点「重试」看起来没反应等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

Pi 新增 Mistral AI 预设

Pi 新增 Mistral AI 预设，走 Pi 自己的 Mistral 协议（mistral-conversations），流式的思考内容不会丢（需求见 #9343，#9344）。

  • 自带 Mistral Large 4 和 Mistral Small 4，思考档位与 Pi 内置的 Mistral 目录一致。
  • 供应商名为 cc-switch-mistral，不会遮住 Pi 内置的 mistral。
  • Pi 表单的协议列表里也可以选 mistral-conversations。Oh My Pi 和 Kimi Code 不支持这个协议，不显示这个预设。

变更

  • 读不出来的供应商和 MCP 条目原样保留，可以单独删除（#9767）：以前数据读不出来时会被当成空配置，下次保存就把默认值写回去，原来的数据丢了。现在这样的条目在供应商页和 MCP 页单独列出，显示读不出来的原因，不能编辑或启用，可以删除（只删 CC Switch 里的记录，不动各应用的配置文件）；其他条目照常显示和使用，故障转移跳过它；改这一条的操作会被拒绝，有这样的条目时，累加式应用（如 OpenCode）把全部供应商同步到配置文件也会报错。升级到能读的版本后即可恢复。
  • Claude Code 的 1M 标记改写成小写 [1m]（见 #9757）：和 Claude Code 文档写法一致。Claude Code 本来不区分大小写，已有的 [1M] 照常可用。
  • 韩语界面翻译改进（#9395，#9349）。
  • 授权中心的账号行更好读（#9369）：「N 个供应商在用」单独一行，额度名称列按最长的名称定宽，窗口窄时套餐、域名和登录日期不再被挤成省略号；德语、俄语等较长的翻译也能看到百分比。

修复

  • 本地路由的监听地址填 IPv6（如 ::1）时启动不了（见 #9743）：以前会报「invalid socket address syntax」，现在可以正常监听。
  • 开了「应用到 Claude Code 插件」后，损坏的 ~/.claude/config.json 会被清空（见 #9744）：文件不是合法 JSON 时，以前当成空文件重写，里面的其他设置都丢了。现在会报错并指出文件路径，文件原样不动；写入也改成先写临时文件再替换，中途中断不会留下半截文件。
  • 网速慢时，大的技能仓库下载到一半被中断：安装、更新、检查更新和发现技能以前都限制整个下载 60 秒。现在只有连续 60 秒收不到数据才中止，并提示「没有收到数据」；整个下载最长 10 分钟。
  • 从访达打开 CC Switch 时，Codex 模型目录探测找不到 Node（见 #9764）：用 npm 装的 Codex 因此运行失败，CC Switch 退回用 Codex 桌面版自带、可能较旧的 CLI，聚合模式下的 GPT 模型少了官方信息（比如搜索工具）。现在探测时带上登录 shell 的 PATH，和 CC Switch 运行其他命令行工具时一样。
  • 工具调用参数里 _ 开头的字段被删掉后才发给上游（#9753）：MCP、数据库、GraphQL 类工具常用 _id、_meta、__typename 这类字段，以前会被当成私有字段删掉，模型看到的调用和实际执行的不一样，严格的供应商可能直接拒绝请求。现在工具调用参数原样保留。
  • 从故障转移队列移出再加回的供应商，卡片显示正常但仍会被跳过一段时间（#9750）：移出队列或退出某个应用的路由时，现在会同时清掉运行中的熔断状态。开启 OMO / OMO Slim 时没能删掉另一个变体的配置文件，现在也会单独提示。
  • 插件页加载超时后点「重试」看起来没反应（见 #9758）：已安装、发现和市场三个页签在重试时都会显示加载中。
  • 插件页的几处问题（#9742）：切到别的应用再切回来，进行中的操作仍显示转圈；Codex 更新插件期间又把它启用，更新结束后不会再被停用；搜索也能按显示名搜到（Pi 通过 git 或本地目录安装的包）；卸掉固定版本的 Pi 包、再从「发现」安装后，它不再留在「发现」里；Windows 上装在 WSL 里的插件现在能显示版本、名称和描述。
  • 内存和资源占用（#9773）：离开会话阅读器时释放缓存的图片；关闭自动更新时释放已下载的安装包；部分网络请求复用 HTTP 连接，不再每次新建；本地路由收到的请求体（含解压后）上限为 200 MiB，超过返回 413。
  • 安装或升级命令行工具后，紧接着的操作偶尔提示「正在进行中」（#9775）。

致谢

感谢 @SaladDay（读不出来的条目原样保留、熔断状态清理、工具锁释放顺序、清理无用代码）、@vastStage（工具调用参数保留 _ 字段）、@funkpopo（插件页修复、授权中心账号行）、@alterxyz（Pi 的 Mistral AI 预设）、@Owlbay（内存和资源占用）、@allenxu09（授权中心账号行）、@cuiyf5516（韩语翻译）的贡献。

感谢 @vastStage、@xxy9468615、@tanghululalala、@JohnZhong505 报告问题和提供复现信息。

完整的技术细节请见 CHANGELOG。

cua-driver-rs-v0.56.4cua-driver-rs: v0.56.4 Prerelease2026-11-21T09:58:50Z

Cua Driver 0.56.4

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • restore macOS web type_text keys and unhide reopened apps. (#8434)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

HyperFrames v0.9.77

Released on 2026-11-21.

CLI messages now show control characters as plain text. An undo in Studio never deletes a file another page still uses, and big root previews no longer hold up other requests.

Features

  • Studio Server: A host can name paths its project history never records (9bea44695, #8224).

Fixes

  • Core: Look up SVG selector aliases by namespace before matching their values (c8c5214a4, #8354).
  • CI: A diff is a release only when it bumps versions (de63e9731, #8355).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in four more commands' printed lines (a744f5c3f, #8346).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in cloudrun and cloud printed lines (af0664c8f, #8350).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in the lambda commands' printed lines (eaec4f4ad, #8348).
  • CLI: Show control characters as text in sign-in, account status and preview lines (7a91dc9fe, #8347).
  • CLI: Show control characters in more command messages as text (9937baee1, #8344).
  • CLI: Show control characters in service reply messages as text (efd04c3b8, #8345).
  • Core: Remember each minted svg id's next free suffix (990aec78b, #8285).
  • Studio Server: An undo never deletes a file a page it leaves still names (7df3ae0a5, #8186).

Performance

  • Studio Server: A root preview's parse passes run off the request thread (e1b951586, #7934).

Docs & Examples

  • Release: List the Layers panel undo fix in the v0.9.75 notes (4095b7368, #8351).

v0.9.74 Stable2026-11-21T02:27:20Z

HyperFrames v0.9.74

Released on 2026-11-21.

Films whose later scene sets an !important background on an id no longer render blank. Clips dropped on a sealed preview show on the timeline, and a package that fails to publish is tried again.

Features

  • Core: A sealed preview's frame runs an in-place text editing session for Studio (9848647b9, #8181).

Fixes

  • Producer: Count composition ids once for nested hosts, and charge the entry page's elements (acdf9e59b, #8335).
  • Ci: A package that fails to publish is tried again before the release fails (55cfbe008, #8333).
  • Core: Keep a later scene's id-selected !important layer hidden before it starts (861046f50, #8331).
  • Producer: A compile's downloads stop when the compile returns, however it ends (1ce69fd81, #8321).
  • Studio: A clip dropped on a sealed preview shows on the timeline (ca91e800e, #8330).
  • Skills: Agents run the CLI through the launcher, never a project's scripts (b2c18a45c, #8326).
  • Media Use: Media writes replace a link at the output name instead of following it (9e3fb696c, #8324).
  • CLI: A model name that every object carries is an unknown model (2ed4a141e, #8319).
  • Share: Hold an unprompted share in a received folder to the review it read (0012c1cb8, #8322).
  • Producer: Pass the render's cancel to probes of a source read at its URL (f8c90591b, #8312).

Internal

  • Engine: The egress proxy test makes its certificate in-process, not with openssl (cc453b767, #8296).

v0.9.73 Stable2026-11-20T23:15:35Z

HyperFrames v0.9.73

Released on 2026-11-20.

Preview copies of long clips with sound finish again, so their footage no longer shows black. Saving history on a drive that stops answering no longer freezes the app, and sealed films show when their timeline arrives late.

Fixes

  • Studio: Paste refuses an element with an event handler or a script link (7b2c6dc1a, #8298).
  • Studio: The first edit of a film without ids no longer reloads the preview (99fcdfb5c, #8274).
  • Producer: Reserve each font's inline size before reading it (8ad346357, #8302).
  • Producer: Refuse a render whose mounts copy more than 100,000 elements (31959db98, #8295).
  • Studio: A timing drag moves the animation in place in a sealed preview (6cf81c668, #8163).
  • Media Use: Never write recipe files through links (be3d21807, #8257).
  • Studio: A host can own the live preview's load deadline, so a slow sealed film isn't an error (44d805c44, #8206).
  • Producer: Download at most 1,000 remote assets per compile, 8 at a time (c50ec09d2, #8290).
  • Producer: Pass the render's cancel to the compile's media, image and font downloads (edd1c90a4, #8289).

cline/cline ↗ 4 releases · 4 stable
v4.1.27 Stable2026-11-21T06:05:04Z

Added

  • LM Studio settings have an API key field, for servers with "Require Authentication" turned on.
  • The MCP tab shows a "read-only" badge on tools whose server declares them read-only.
  • The Account view has a Privacy settings link to the Cline web app's data-use preferences.
  • The extension package now includes its third-party license notices.

Changed

  • With no saved model, the Cline provider now starts on the first free model instead of Claude Sonnet 5.5. Saved selections are untouched.
  • The reasoning effort dropdown shows the effort a new task will actually use, and has a Default entry that clears the saved setting.
  • A provider's model list refreshes after you save its API key or base URL, and a failed refresh (such as a rejected key) shows as an error in the model picker.
  • read_files results are no longer cut to 8k characters on the way to the model. A read now arrives in full, up to the tool's own limit of 2000 lines or about 48k characters.
  • Refreshed the model catalog. Adds 94 providers from models.dev and drops Lilac and Tempr. The Cline recommended list adds Muse Ember. The Cline free list drops Reflection Beam, Laguna S 2.1 and Solar Mini 4. ClinePass drops DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, Mimo V2.6 Flash, Mimo V2.6 Pro, MiniMax M3, Qwen3.7 Plus and Qwen3.7 Max. Default models change for 67 other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, Gemini 4 Neon (including the gemini and vertex providers) or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017.

Fixed

  • Picking "None" for reasoning effort is now saved and sent. Before, GPT-6 on Azure kept its default effort and rejected tool calls.
  • Turning reasoning off now reaches custom models that have saved Model Configuration overrides. Before, gpt-5.6 and gpt-6 chat-completions endpoints rejected every tool call.
  • On the native OpenAI provider, the reasoning level is fitted to what each model supports, so max and Extra High are no longer rejected by GPT-5.x and o-series models.
  • Reflection Beam no longer fails every turn with 400 Missing required parameter: 'messages[n].reasoning_content' after a compaction or a model switch.
  • Compaction no longer falls back to the basic summary on models the catalog doesn't describe.
  • LiteLLM: the legacy max_tokens field is no longer used as the input budget when the model reports max_output_tokens.
  • Reasoning text from a turn that was cancelled or failed no longer shows up at the start of the next turn's reasoning.
  • Commit search passes your query to git as arguments instead of shell text, so a quoted search matches the exact phrase.
  • Security: undici raised to 7.30.1, which fixes two process-crash issues.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/v4.1.26...v4.1.27

View release →

  • One-shot runs (a prompt argument or piped stdin) now start in yolo mode instead of act mode, since nobody is at the keyboard to approve tools. Act mode is kept when auto-approve is off (--auto-approve false or the saved setting), when a mode flag is passed, when plan mode is saved, or when the prompt is a /team prompt. Yolo mode has no team tools, so pass --act when you need them
  • --timeout and Ctrl+C now stop the first turn of a one-shot run. Before, neither could interrupt it
  • The CLI no longer reads .env or bunfig.toml from the directory it is launched in
  • With no saved model, the Cline provider now starts on the first free model instead of Claude Sonnet 5.5. Saved selections are untouched

sdk/sdk/v0.2.1SDK v0.2.1 Stable2026-11-21T05:19:22Z
  • read_files results are no longer re-cut to the 8k tool-result cap on the way to the provider. They use the 50k file-content cap, so a read the tool reported in full arrives in full. Command, search, MCP and custom tool results keep the 8k cap, and an explicit tool-result cap still applies
  • Models that need interleaved reasoning get a placeholder reasoning_content on assistant messages that have none. This fixes Reflection Beam's 400 Missing required parameter: 'messages[n].reasoning_content' after compaction or a model switch. The fact is carried as ModelInfo.metadata.interleavedReasoningField
  • The summarizer now states a preference to go without reasoning instead of disabling it outright. The gateway disables it only for models whose catalog entry advertises an off option. Before, uncatalogued models got reasoning_effort: "none", which some endpoints reject, and compaction fell back to basic
  • A custom model with saved overrides now has reasoning seeded into its capability list unless it sets supportsReasoning: false, so an explicit reasoning disable reaches the provider. Before, gpt-5.6 and gpt-6 chat-completions endpoints rejected every tool call
  • The native OpenAI adapter fits the reasoning level to the model's advertised effort ladder, so max and Extra High are no longer sent verbatim to GPT-5.x and o-series models that reject them
  • LiteLLM: the legacy max_tokens is no longer used as the input budget when max_output_tokens is set. Null limits are treated as missing
  • The cline provider's bundled default is now the first bundled free model. resolveClineDefaultModelId() in @cline/core returns the first free model from the live recommended feed, then the bundled one, then CLINE_DEFAULT_MODEL_ID
  • CLINE_DEFAULT_MODEL_ID is now anthropic/claude-sonnet-5.5, and the remaining hardcoded default-model literals use it. @cline/shared adds ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_MODEL_ID for Anthropic's bare id format
  • A running compaction can be stopped, and cancellations are counted in telemetry
  • Session config and the hub RPC schema accept the max reasoning effort
  • With tools and spawning enabled, core registers ux_verifier, which delegates an ordered checklist to a subagent through the host's spawn_agent executor. Disabling spawn_agent disables it too
  • createUxVerificationReportTool() creates the opt-in report_ux_verification tool for structured reports with captions, pass/fail/blocked results and screenshot references
  • createPublishArtifactsTool({ publish }) creates the opt-in publish_artifacts tool. createArtifactPublisher from @cline/core/cloud publishes to cloud sessions through signed upload policies. Artifact IDs default to the source filename
  • McpToolDescriptor.annotations carries a server's tool hints (readOnlyHint and friends) through the stdio and URL clients and the manager cache. task.tool_used telemetry gains mode
  • session.resume starts a runtime for a saved session ID and reads history on the hub, instead of requiring initialMessages
  • Cloud task creation accepts initialMessage to create and send in one call, and localGit to launch from a captured Git snapshot
  • HubCloudSessionClient.getArtifactDownloadUrl(sessionId, artifactId) returns a signed URL without starting the sandbox
  • Provider, model, MCP and MCP OAuth management are available as hub commands, with token authentication required for provider management
  • The hub can return session history in bounded chunks and archived cloud history
  • The hub validates caller accounts for cloud commands, and no longer re-sends a rejected Cline refresh token on every cloud poll
  • Hub cloud requests no longer duplicate the API prefix
  • Hub session handlers are retained when a session deletion fails
  • Confirmed cloud handoff publication is exported from @cline/core/cloud for direct hosts
  • @cline/shared adds resolveClineAppUrl, and the account response carries dataPrivacyPath for the web app's privacy settings page
  • @cline/ui adds SectionHeading, Banner, ItemCard, DetailList, controlled Dialog components and a UX verification report carousel with screenshot downloads. Buttons, badges and switches are refreshed (loading and pending states, a small switch size), along with the colour tokens and a new text-xxs step
  • Removed the CLINE_CAPTURE_* provider request capture, which wrote full request bodies to disk
  • Every SDK package now includes its license file
  • Refreshed the model catalog (6,457 → 7,621 models). Adds 94 providers from models.dev and drops Lilac and Tempr. The Cline recommended list adds Muse Ember. The Cline free list drops Reflection Beam, Laguna S 2.1 and Solar Mini 4, so the cline provider's bundled default is Nemotron 3.5 Super VL. ClinePass drops DeepSeek V4.1 Flash, Mimo V2.6 Flash, Mimo V2.6 Pro, MiniMax M3, Qwen3.7 Plus and Qwen3.7 Max. Default models change for 67 other providers, mostly to Kimi K3.1 Pro, Gemini 4 Neon (including the gemini and vertex providers) or DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017. Qwen models now report tiered families such as qwen-plus and qwen-max

Full Changelog: https://github.com/cline/cline/compare/sdk/sdk/v0.2.0...sdk/sdk/v0.2.1

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-21T05:16:33Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2152+gf2f87f5fc5
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1795119671

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

stablyai/orca ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v1.4.268 Stable2026-11-21T03:50:43Z

We shipped more than one release today. Check out 1.4.267 and 1.4.266 too.

Bots on more agents, richer Slack & a faster Orca ✨

Bots can now run on Cursor, GitHub Copilot and Hermes, Slack gets scheduling and canvases, and Orca does less work in the background so it feels quicker.

  • 🤖 Run bots on more agents: Bots can run on Cursor, GitHub Copilot and Hermes, and OpenCode 2.x bots get their standing instructions. New bots get an identity card, and Soul & Memory have redesigned editors.
  • 🖼️ Send images and files: Attach images and files in bot chats, group chats and item comments, including to a bot on a paired computer. Images on a Slack request reach the agent as images.
  • 💬 More from Slack: orca slack post --at schedules a post for later, and new pin, canvas, list and scheduled commands cover pins, canvases and lists. Connecting Slack takes the copied token without extra clicks, and mentions read as names.
  • ⚡ A lighter, smoother Orca: Animations run smoothly again instead of about once a second, spinners and pulses draw fewer frames, credential reads move off the main thread, and automatic heap profiles are gone. Orca also suggests cleaning up workspaces when the disk is almost full.
  • 🧭 More reliable workers and chats: Chats cut by a crash or restart can continue, a worker's final report wakes the agent that started it, and worker names in chat open the worker. Chat also gets Antigravity, Claude, Codex and Droid fixes.
  • 🌐 Annotate pages on remote hosts: Browser annotations now work on remote hosts too.

About Orca Bots (Beta) <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="36" height="36" valign="middle" />

Orca Bots work like first-class teammates: every managed agent session gets its tools and identity, bots can share on the owner's behalf, they follow Slack threads and Gmail triggers more reliably, and connecting them is clearer and safer.

  • <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="28" height="28" valign="middle" /> Every bot session gets its tools and identity: Managed agent sessions are no longer limited to Claude and Codex; each one starts with the tools and identity it needs.
  • 🔗 Bots can share, just like the owner: Agent CLI callers can use Sharing the same way the owner does.
  • 💬 Slack threads show what a bot is doing: A thread now reflects what its turn is working on, and long answers arrive whole instead of cut up.
  • 📬 Gmail triggers match the real sender: Triggers match the sender's actual address, not a display name that can differ.
  • 🛡️ Clearer access warnings: The access confirm dialog now spells out what you are allowing before you connect a bot.

Coming soon

  • Antigravity bug fixes
  • More connectors
  • Easier moving of bots between different machines
  • More performance improvements
  • Fix bots stall issues under heavy task load
  • Improved PR review workflow
  • More plugin API
  • Sidebar UX improvements

What's Changed

🤖 Bots, connectors & access

Bots can run on Cursor, GitHub Copilot and Hermes, get redesigned Soul & Memory editors and a new-bot identity card, and take images and files in chats and item comments. Slack gains scheduled posts, pins, canvases and a smoother setup, and paired servers get clearer sign-in.

v1.4.267 Stable2026-11-20T21:31:37Z

A quick patch on 1.4.266 with stability and UI improvements.

What's new in 1.4.267

  • 🛠️ Steadier status bar: Stability improvements. by @brennanb2025 in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/44647
  • 💬 Smoother chat: Finished turns now settle cleanly. by @AmethystLiang in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/45817
  • 🤖 Cleaner Bots card: The sidebar intro card is simpler and fits any width. by @AmethystLiang in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/45442

Introducing Orca Bots (Beta) <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="36" height="36" valign="middle" />

Orca Bots work like first-class teammates: every managed agent session gets its tools and identity, bots can share on the owner's behalf, they follow Slack threads and Gmail triggers more reliably, and connecting them is clearer and safer.

  • <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/stablyai/orca/main/src/shared/bot-mascots/A1.webp" alt="Orca" width="28" height="28" valign="middle" /> Every bot session gets its tools and identity: Managed agent sessions are no longer limited to Claude and Codex; each one starts with the tools and identity it needs.
  • 🔗 Bots can share, just like the owner: Agent CLI callers can use Sharing the same way the owner does.
  • 💬 Slack threads show what a bot is doing: A thread now reflects what its turn is working on, and long answers arrive whole instead of cut up.
  • 📬 Gmail triggers match the real sender: Triggers match the sender's actual address, not a display name that can differ.
  • 🛡️ Clearer access warnings: The access confirm dialog now spells out what you are allowing before you connect a bot.

Everything else in 1.4.266 is unchanged. See the 1.4.266 release notes for the full list.

tw93/Pake ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
V3.20.0V3.20.0 Keyring Stable2026-11-21T02:16:54Z

<div align="center"> <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Pake/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Pake Logo" width="120" height="120" style="border-radius:50%" /> <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Pake V3.20.0</h1> <p><em>Turn any webpage into a desktop app with one command.</em></p> </div>

Changelog

  1. On macOS, apps can sign in with passkeys. Each app keeps its own in the Mac's Secure Enclave, behind Touch ID or the login password, so sign in another way once and add a passkey in the site's settings. Apps made with --incognito or --ignore-certificate-errors keep none.
  2. On macOS, a player's fullscreen button now fills the screen, as on YouTube, where the video stayed a small box.
  3. Google sign-in on X, TikTok and Notion now finishes on macOS and Windows: the sign-in popup opens as its own window and closes when it is done.
  4. On macOS, a page's alert(), confirm() and prompt() show as dialogs, so a site that asks before logging out or deleting works.
  5. DeepSeek no longer shows "Abnormal usage environment". Sites no longer see window.__TAURI__, while your own --inject script still gets it, with core.invoke, shell.open and the window commands.
  6. With --new-window, a link the page opens itself no longer opens in the system browser as well, as Douyin's episode list did.
  7. On macOS, a page that went white because its web process died reloads by itself, and quitting such an app no longer leaves a crash report.
  8. On Linux, the title bar is dark on a dark desktop such as Omarchy, where it stayed light over a dark page.
  9. On Windows and Linux, Ctrl+Shift+H goes back to the app's home page. A page that uses the key itself still gets it.
  10. An --inject file that is not .css or .js, or that does not exist, now stops the build with an error instead of building an app without its proxy, download folder, multi-window and other settings.

更新日志

  1. macOS 上打包出来的应用可以用通行密钥登录了，每个应用的通行密钥存在这台 Mac 的安全隔区里，用触控 ID 或开机密码确认，先用别的方式登录一次，再到网站设置里添加通行密钥，带 --incognito 或 --ignore-certificate-errors 的应用不保存。
  2. macOS 上点播放器的全屏按钮，视频现在会铺满屏幕，之前 YouTube 上窗口全屏了视频还是一个小框。
  3. X、TikTok 和 Notion 上用 Google 登录在 macOS 和 Windows 上能走完了，登录弹窗会单独开一个窗口，登录完自己关掉。
  4. macOS 上网页的 alert()、confirm() 和 prompt() 会弹出对话框，退出登录或删除前要确认的网站可以正常用了。
  5. DeepSeek 不再提示“使用环境异常”，网站现在读不到 window.__TAURI__，你用 --inject 注入的脚本照旧能用它的 core.invoke、shell.open 和窗口命令。
  6. 开了 --new-window 时，网页自己打开的链接不会再同时在系统浏览器里打开一份，比如抖音的选集列表。
  7. macOS 上网页进程崩溃变白后会自己重新加载，这时退出应用也不会再留下崩溃报告。
  8. Linux 上在 Omarchy 这类深色桌面里，窗口标题栏现在也是深色，之前是深色网页上顶着一条浅色标题栏。
  9. Windows 和 Linux 上按 Ctrl+Shift+H 回到应用的首页，网页自己用到这个按键时照旧交给网页。
  10. --inject 传了不是 .css 或 .js 的文件，或者文件不存在，现在会直接报错停止，之前会照常打包但悄悄丢掉代理、下载目录、多窗口等设置。

Special thanks to @thishakyawmh, @visveshkhanna, and @MalikHw for the pull requests behind this release. If Pake helps you, please consider giving it a star and recommending it to your friends.

Pake App

If you would rather click than type commands, Pake App makes apps from a ready-made gallery or any URL on Mac, Windows and Linux, with no Node.js or Rust to install, and adds ad blocking and updates for the apps you made. You can download it here. The CLI stays free and open source.

https://github.com/tw93/Pake

pip install 'browser-use==1.0.8rc1'

  • The judge asks as one more turn of the run, so the provider reads the run from its cache instead of billing the trace again. It returns the same verdict fields, gotchas included (#6457).
  • No API key over SSH or on a headless machine: the run prints a link and a code to approve on any device, then continues (#6427).

gastownhall/beads ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v1.4.0-rc.4 Prerelease2026-11-20T21:56:01Z

beads v1.4.0-rc.4

Pre-compiled binaries for Linux, macOS (Intel & Apple Silicon), Windows (AMD64 & ARM64), Android/Termux (ARM64), and FreeBSD.

Installation (release candidate)

This is a prerelease for validation. The stable install channels — Homebrew, the install.sh / install.ps1 scripts, npm, and PyPI — intentionally keep serving the latest stable release, so they will not install this RC. Use one of the methods below.

Manual download (recommended): Download the archive for your platform from the Assets below, extract it, and place bd on your PATH.

Go install (pinned to this RC):

go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.4
# For embedded Dolt support:
CGO_ENABLED=1 GOFLAGS=-tags=gms_pure_go go install github.com/steveyegge/beads/cmd/bd@v1.4.0-rc.4

1.4.0 is a one-way upgrade. Back up first, with your current bd. Consenting to the schema migration (bd migrate schema, --force, or BD_ALLOW_MIGRATE=1) moves a 1.2.x or 1.3.x store to schema v74. After that, bd 1.3.x and older refuse the database; going back means restoring a backup. From 1.4.0-rc.1, rc.2 or rc.3, rc.4 is a binary swap: it adds no migration.

  • Validate on a copy, or on a store you have backed up with your current bd (bd dolt push, a JSONL export, or bd backup sync).
  • Every Dolt server bd talks to, and the dolt CLI on PATH, must be 2.6.0 or newer.
  • Read Upgrade notes and Known issues under [1.4.0-rc.4], [1.4.0-rc.3], [1.4.0-rc.2] and [1.4.0-rc.1] in CHANGELOG.md before installing.

New in rc.4:

  • Go programs that link beads can open a proxied-server workspace with beads.OpenBestAvailable (#8323). It used to return no error and open a new, empty embedded store at .beads/embeddeddolt there (cgo builds). It now goes through the workspace's bd proxy: it adopts a running proxy or starts one from the bd on PATH; with no bd there, or with a proxy that a bd older than 1.4 started (left running), it fails with beads.ErrProxyUnavailable. A managed-local workspace also needs dolt on PATH (or BEADS_DOLT_BIN), even to adopt a running proxy. Forcing server mode and naming a plain Dolt server's port in BEADS_DOLT_SERVER_PORT keeps the direct server connection (a 1.4 proxy's data port accepts only bd). The returned store does not serve every Storage method; see [1.4.0-rc.4] for its differences from DoltStore. beads.Open, OpenFromConfig and OpenGated are unchanged (see Still open).
  • On a proxied-server workspace whose database has a Dolt remote (bd init --proxied-server adds one from git origin by default), the remote-migrate refusal no longer offers bd migrate --force, BD_ALLOW_REMOTE_MIGRATE=1 bd migrate, bd dolt push, bd dolt pull or bd bootstrap, which are refused there or create a separate local database. It names bd migrate schema --force, run on one clone of that remote only with the proxy's library clients stopped (#8568). Server and embedded mode are unchanged.
  • On a shared or external Dolt server and in proxied-server workspaces, the refusals a 1.3.x upgrade meets, and the bd upgraded to …: N schema migration(s) pending notice, no longer say read commands keep working until you migrate: every issue read needs arrival_seq (migration 0073), so until bd migrate schema runs an upgraded client can neither read nor write issues. Upgrade every client and migrate in one sitting (#8571).

From rc.3:

  • Telemetry no longer exports issue content. Correction to the rc.1 notes: with the console trace exporter on (BD_OTEL_ENABLED=true with OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER=console, or the legacy BD_OTEL_STDOUT=true on its own), rc.1 and rc.2 still wrote titles, descriptions, notes, labels, comments and search text to stderr in the bd.args span attribute (#8505); logs that kept bd's stderr can hold it. bd.args now names only the command and the flags that were set, so trace queries that read its values must change.
  • bd ranks a release candidate below its release (#6595). Moving from a candidate to 1.4.0 records the release in the version marker in proxied-server workspaces and on a Dolt server whose data is in .beads/dolt; in shared-server mode and on other external servers bd doctor keeps reporting the candidate until you run bare bd migrate once per database.

Behavior changes to know before installing (from rc.2):

  • bd github now stops when a repository lookup fails. Rerun after a temporary failure. Never clear or repoint another repository's bead links to get past it: the next sync creates unlinked beads as new issues in the configured repository.
  • Server-mode pulls need EXECUTE on dolt_fetch and dolt_merge.
  • bd backup restore refuses a Dolt server on another host. Run it on the server's machine, in a workspace that names the same dolt_database.

Still open:

  • #7283: after bd migrate schema the recorded bd version can stay at 1.3.x in every mode; in server mode bd doctor reports it — run bare bd migrate once per database (embedded too; proxied-server mode refuses it and nothing there acts on the marker). See the corrections under [1.4.0-rc.4] Known issues.
  • From 1.3.x, bd ready prints no upgrade notice and bd upgrade status says upgraded: false: read these notes, or run bd info --whats-new after bd migrate schema (bd refuses it before).
  • Go programs that open a proxied-server workspace with beads.Open, OpenFromConfig or OpenGated still go around its proxy: Open can create an empty database, and with no proxy running the other two start a Dolt server that blocks bd until it is killed. Use OpenBestAvailable (#8412, #8323).
  • On a proxied-server workspace, no bd command adopts a remote that another clone has already migrated, and the adopt and fork stops still name bd bootstrap (which creates a separate local database there). bd can't refresh its copy of the remote in that mode, so check with the other clones before you migrate one. See Known issues under [1.4.0-rc.4] for the manual adopt route.
  • Only bd can use a proxied-server workspace's proxy data port (since rc.1). A plain MySQL client, or a gc release that reads a proxied rig directly, is closed after 5 seconds with nothing sent (ERROR 2013 (HY000): Lost connection to server at 'handshake: reading initial communication packet'), and proxy.log records it only if the proxy was started with BD_DEBUG=1. Run SQL with bd sql.
  • bd create --repo <url>'s cached clone still writes the URL into merge commits (#8434).

When 1.4.0 ships, install it by hand. Server-mode bd doctor on rc.3 or rc.4, run in a terminal, also tells you; nothing tells you on rc.1 or rc.2, on an embedded or proxied-server workspace, or when bd doctor runs under an agent, a script or --json.

Changelog

Features

  • ef08cbaf659466ea4e2cd3ee5f623d32826d377e feat(sdk): route proxied-server workspaces through the bd proxy (GH#8323) (#8558) (#8563)

Bug Fixes

  • 89b5bfe0e7af3536d694c3ff960f9b1708864dd5 fix(migrate): give proxied-server workspaces a remote-migrate refusal they can act on (bd-5hl9dg) (#8568)
  • b97fce2a56336e94e76fe37d927d93e4a789c843 fix(migrate): stop promising that reads keep working before a schema migration (#8571)
  • 7c0e969e269d08e19781f4af87c6a52a78615622 fix(uow): restore #8571's readOnly comment hunk dropped in the backport resolution

Others

  • 5cd368a4ba624cc3c99fe415cf7515d3fbc7b734 chore(release): fold #8568 into v1.4.0-rc.4 notes; date rc.4 2026-11-20
  • cdd563ce00235a34dc8f252407ce900c1dde9dc2 chore(release): fold #8571 into v1.4.0-rc.4 notes; correct earlier rc notes from the upgrade drill
  • cde822f3895c84e546fdbe4312f14ce2ea7ba97d chore(release): prepare v1.4.0-rc.4
  • f7e572f8495839a41e1d34628e21bcbdbc339490 docs(release): correct rc.4 adopt known issue and --force guidance (#8569 review)

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.114graphify 0.9.114 Stable2026-11-20T21:22:45Z

Performance work on the extract/build/update pipeline, plus a batch of extractor and build fixes. Every entry marked Perf preserves graph output exactly (byte-identical); the extractor fixes change output and share one AST cache schema bump (see the note at the end).

  • Perf: ids normalize_id gets an ASCII fast-path, pre-compiled regexes and an LRU cache, so the common (ASCII, repeated) identifier case skips the Unicode normalization loop. ~1.26x on the compute path and a large win on repeated ids, which recurs heavily across a corpus.
  • Perf: cache save_cached builds the portable on-disk payload copy-on-write instead of copy.deepcopy-ing every per-file result; ~20% faster on the cache-write transform, scaling with per-file graph size. The input is never mutated and the on-disk bytes are identical.
  • Perf: extract each source file is read from disk once (the key hash and the parse share one buffer) instead of twice on a cold build, and the tree-sitter Parser is reused per language within a worker instead of rebuilt per file (file reads roughly halved on the worker path).
  • Perf: extract the cross-file resolution finalize builds its shared node/edge indexes in a few fused passes instead of ~6 independent full scans of the graph; ~15% faster on the finalize stage.
  • Perf: watch the no-change topology comparison uses an order-independent content hash computed in one pass instead of sorting and re-serializing the whole graph; ~1.4x on that compare.
  • Perf: cluster the community partition is cached by a topology hash and reused when the graph is unchanged (~9x on a cluster cache hit), and the deterministic edge-sort key drops a per-edge json.dumps in favour of a cheap tuple key (identical ordering).
  • Perf: analyze suggest_questions' betweenness centrality now runs a flat-array Brandes implementation instead of networkx's dict-based one, with bit-for-bit identical scores; ~2.6x faster, growing with graph size (#5303, thanks @Max17190).
  • Perf: watch each preserved source path is rebased once per incremental update instead of recomputed per node and edge; the update output is byte-identical (#5322, thanks @Max17190).
  • Fix: java a field or class annotated with Lombok @Getter gets the synthesized getX()/isX() getter node javac sees, so obj.getX() resolves to it; boolean fields use isX, @Getter(AccessLevel.NONE) is respected, a hand-written getter is not duplicated, and a non-Lombok @Getter is ignored (#5148, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: kotlin a member call on a receiver with a declared type (parameter, property, val x: T, or val x = T()) binds to that type's method across files instead of the last same-named method in the file, and interface calls dispatch to their implementers; an ambiguous or unknown receiver fails closed (#5355, thanks @xiongjianxu).
  • Fix: sql a PostgreSQL PL/pgSQL routine body that the grammar only parses through ERROR recovery (RETURN QUERY SELECT ... FROM t) has its table reads recovered as reads_from edges instead of dropped (#5349, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: build when an AST-extracted edge and a semantic (LLM) copy of the same edge tie, the deterministic EXTRACTED edge is kept, so a later docs --update no longer deletes a code edge that a semantic copy had overwritten (#5334, thanks @Ildar-gn).
  • Fix: sql a T-SQL procedure whose header the grammar only partly parses (tree-sitter-sql 0.3.12+ misreads the name, absorbing unparenthesised params or a ""/]] escape) yields a single routine node instead of a duplicate under the misread id; a real routine sharing the misread's line is kept (#5236, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: vbnet a method declared after a multi-line string literal is no longer dropped: the file is reparsed with multi-line strings flattened to a line-preserving placeholder first, so an unterminated string on one line no longer swallows the rest of the class; single-line files are parsed unchanged (#5320, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).
  • Fix: cpp a std::-qualified type (std::chrono::seconds, std::vector<T>) is no longer rewired onto a same-named project symbol; the std reference gets its own sourceless stub the corpus rewire skips, while the std type's template arguments still resolve to the project's own types (#5219, thanks @deba2k5).
  • Fix: python a plain import mod where mod sits on a sys.path.insert(...) directory (a non-package script dir) now binds mod.func() to the func function node, matched to the unique file on that path and failing closed on ambiguity or a stdlib name; a calls edge is no longer aliased onto a file node (#5253, thanks @madhusudanpatnaik).
  • Fix: serve a query that filters on the call relation now keeps cross-repo calls edges (written as relation=calls, context=cross_repo), which were silently dropped by the context-only filter (#5257, thanks @LHopitalOne).
  • Fix: build a member's structural method/contains edge to its owner is kept over an opposite-direction member-usage edge (a method reading its class's own static property or constant), so the member no longer vanishes from the type's walk; non-usage relations (inherits/implements) keep the existing rule (#2391, thanks @livingforelvis).

The AST cache schema is bumped to 50; warm caches re-run the affected Kotlin, Java, SQL, VB.NET, C++ and Python (sys.path import) extractions.
